# Aggregated political leader scripted effects [M-R], merged from per-country files.

# --- MAD_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_MAD = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 14 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Neutral_conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_mad_hery_rajaonarimampianina
				picture = "Hery_Rajaonarimampianina.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_conservatism
				traits = {
					neutrality_Neutral_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# --- MAL_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_MAL = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 2 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { liberalism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_mal_mountaga_tall
				picture = "Mountaga_Tall.dds"
				ideology = liberalism
				traits = {
					western_liberalism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_mal_soumaila_cisse
				picture = "soumaila_cisse.dds"
				ideology = liberalism
				traits = {
					western_liberalism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 3 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { socialism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_mal_ibrahim_boubacar_keita
				picture = "Ibrahim_Boubacar_Keita.dds"
				ideology = socialism
				traits = {
					western_socialism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 22 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_mal_col_pangassy_sangare
				picture = "Col_Pangassy_Sangare.dds"
				ideology = Nat_Autocracy
				traits = {
					nationalist_Nat_Autocracy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_mal_mahamane_toure
				picture = "Portrait_Mahamame_Toure.dds"
				ideology = Nat_Autocracy
				traits = {
					nationalist_Nat_Autocracy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_mal_modibo_sidibe
				picture = "modibo_sidibe.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 4 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { communist_state_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { communist_state_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_mal_oumar_mariko
				picture = "oumar_mariko.dds"
				ideology = communist_state
				traits = {
					emerging_communist_state
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { communist_state_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 18 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { neutral_Social_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { neutral_Social_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_mal_dramane_dembele
				picture = "dramane_dembele.dds"
				ideology = neutral_Social
				traits = {
					neutrality_neutral_Social
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { neutral_Social_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 17 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_green_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Neutral_green_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_mal_dr_bintou_diallo_fadimata
				picture = "bintou_diallo_fadimata.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_green
				traits = {
					neutrality_Neutral_green
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_green_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 14 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Neutral_conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_mal_amadou_toumani_toure
				picture = "amadou_toure.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_conservatism
				traits = {
					neutrality_Neutral_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# --- MAR_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_MAR = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 2 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { liberalism_leader = 0 } }
			set_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 }
			kill_country_leader = yes

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_mar_imata_kabua
				picture = "Imata_Kabua.dds"
				ideology = liberalism
				traits = {
					western_liberalism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 3 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { socialism_leader = 0 } }
			set_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 }
			kill_country_leader = yes

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_mar_hilda_heine
				picture = "Hilda_Heine.dds"
				ideology = socialism
				traits = {
					western_socialism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# --- MAU_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_MAU = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 7 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			set_variable = { Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			kill_country_leader = yes

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_mau_mahfouz_ould_al_azizi
				picture = "Mahfouz_Ould_al_Azizi.dds"
				ideology = Autocracy
				traits = {
					emerging_Autocracy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 14 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			set_variable = { Neutral_conservatism_leader = 1 }
			kill_country_leader = yes

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_mau_mohamed_ould_abdel_aziz
				picture = "Mohamed_Ould_Abdel_Aziz.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_conservatism
				traits = {
					neutrality_Neutral_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# --- MOZ_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_MOZ = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 1 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_moz_afonso_dhlakama
				picture = "afonso_dhlakama.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 2 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { liberalism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_moz_raul_domingos
				picture = "raul_domingos.dds"
				ideology = liberalism
				traits = {
					western_liberalism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 22 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_moz_general_graca_chongo
				picture = "Portrait_Graca_Chongo.dds"

				traits = {
					nationalist_Nat_Autocracy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 14 } }

			add_to_variable = { Neutral_conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_moz_daviz_simango
				picture = "daviz_simango.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_conservatism
				traits = {
					neutrality_Neutral_conservatism

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_conservatism_leader = 1 } }

# --- PAR_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_PAR = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 2 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { liberalism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = PAR_luis_macchi
				picture = "luis_macchi.dds"
				ideology = liberalism
				traits = {
					western_liberalism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
	}
}

# --- PAU_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_PAU = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 2 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { liberalism_leader = 0 } }
			set_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 }
			kill_country_leader = yes

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_pau_kuniwo_nakamura
				picture = "kuniwo_nakamura.dds"
				ideology = liberalism
				traits = {
					western_liberalism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 3 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { socialism_leader = 0 } }
			set_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 }
			kill_country_leader = yes

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_pau_thomas_remengesau_jr
				picture = "thomas_remengesau_jr.dds"
				ideology = socialism
				traits = {
					western_socialism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# --- PER_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_PER = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 2 } }

		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = PER_second_ir } check_variable = { liberalism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_per_reza_pahlavi
				picture = "Western_King_Reza_Pahlavi.dds"
				ideology = liberalism
				traits = {
					king
					western_liberalism
					pro_american
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = PER_second_ir } check_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_per_hamed_esmail
				picture = "hamed_esmaeilion.dds"
				ideology = liberalism
				traits = {
					western_liberalism
					cautious
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = PER_second_ir } check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } }

			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_per_shirin_ebadi
				picture = "shirin_ebadi.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					political_dancer
					sly
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017

				hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

				create_country_leader = {
					name = ZZZ_nm_per_nezameddin_movahed

						western_socialism
						sly
					}
				}

# --- PHI_political_leaders.txt ---
# Author(s): AngriestBird, Chrisonbikyu
# Anti-dynasty flag: PHI_anti_dynasty_law_active

set_leader_PHI = {
	# Lakas–Christian Muslim Democrats (Lakas-CMD)
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 1 } }

		# Counter 0: GMA 1 — always the first leader, no conditions
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }
			create_country_leader = {
				name = PHI_gloria_macapagal_arroyo
				picture = "gfx/leaders/PHI/gloria_macapagal_arroyo.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					deceitful
					teacher
					captain_of_industry
					experienced_banker
					western_conservatism
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}

		# Counter 1: GMA 2 if Erap was ousted, skip to Teodoro if Erap won
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = PHI_edsa_dos_erap_victory } }
				hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }
				create_country_leader = {
					name = PHI_gloria_macapagal_arroyo
					picture = "gfx/leaders/PHI/gloria_macapagal_arroyo.dds"
					ideology = conservatism
					traits = {
						deceitful
						teacher
						captain_of_industry
						experienced_banker
						western_conservatism
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { date < 2010.6.30 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } }
			}
			# PHI_edsa_dos_erap_victory: no leader created, no b set, falls through to Teodoro
		}

		# Counter 2: Teodoro — reached normally after GMA 2, or immediately if Erap won
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 2 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }
			create_country_leader = {
				name = PHI_gilbert_teodoro
				picture = "gfx/leaders/PHI/gilbert_teodoro.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					lawyer
					rational
					cautious
					western_conservatism
				}
			}
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.6.30 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } }
		}

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 3 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = PHI_anti_dynasty_law_active } }
				hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }
				create_country_leader = {
					name = PHI_martin_romualdez
					picture = "gfx/leaders/PHI/martin_romualdez.dds"
					ideology = conservatism
					traits = {
						lawyer
						career_politician
						western_conservatism
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
			}
		}

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 4 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = PHI_anti_dynasty_law_active } }
				hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }
				create_country_leader = {
					name = PHI_bong_revilla
					picture = "gfx/leaders/PHI/bong_revilla.dds"
					ideology = conservatism
					traits = {
						actor_politician
						populist
						western_conservatism
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
			}
		}

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 5 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }
			create_country_leader = {
				name = PHI_mannix_dalipe
				picture = "gfx/leaders/PHI/mannix_dalipe.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					career_politician
					lawyer
					western_conservatism
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 6 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }
			create_country_leader = {
				name = PHI_jose_aquino_ii
				picture = "gfx/leaders/PHI/jose_aquino_ii.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					career_politician
					businessman
					western_conservatism
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 7 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }
			create_country_leader = {

				}
			}

		}
	}

	# Liberal Party (LP)

					nationalist_Nat_Autocracy
				}
			}
			if = { limit = { date < 2022.6.30 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } }

				}

# --- PKK_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_PKK = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 6 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Conservative_leader = 0 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { Conservative_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_pkk_murat_karayilan
				picture = "Murat_Karayilan.dds"
				ideology = Conservative
				traits = {
					emerging_Conservative
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Conservative_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
}

			create_country_leader = {
				name = POR_jose_manuel_durao_barroso
				picture = "Jose_Manuel_Durao_Barroso.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.1 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } }
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

				traits = {
					western_conservatism

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 1 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = PRU_victor_andres_garcia_belaunde
				picture = "victor_andres_garcia.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}

set_leader_PTR = {

    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = set_conservatism }

			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = PTR_luis_fortuno
				picture = "LuisFortunao.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.11.1 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2001
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } }}
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

				name = PTR_wanda_vazquez
				picture = "wanda_vazquez.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } 
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 2 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } }}
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

set_leader_PUN = {
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 13 } }

            limit = { date > 2019.1.7 }
            create_country_leader = {
                name = PUN_said_abdullahi_deni
                picture = "gfx/leaders/generic_politicians/black_004.dds"
                ideology = Neutral_Autocracy
                traits = {
                    neutrality_Neutral_Autocracy
                }
            }
        }
        else = {
            create_country_leader = {
                name = PUN_abdiweli_mohamed_ali_gaas
                picture = "gfx/leaders/generic_politicians/black_001.dds"
                ideology = Neutral_Autocracy
                traits = {
                    neutrality_Neutral_Autocracy
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { communist_state_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { communist_state_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

				name = ROM_aurelian_pavelescu

# --- RWA_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_RWA = {
	if = { limit = { western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_rwa_pasteur_bizimungu
				picture = "RWA_Pasteur_Bizimungu.dds"
				ideology = Western_Autocracy
				traits = {
					western_Western_Autocracy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
	}
}
